([succtorlin/use-case](https://github.com/succtorlin/use-case/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★1)
- **use-case**: Writes a use case specification: one normative, implementation-agnostic document per user goal, with actors, guarantees, the main success scenario, and extensions. Not for casual feature brainstorming that hasn't become an intent to specify. ([wuersch/adlc](https://github.com/wuersch/adlc/tree/HEAD/plugins/adlc/skills/use-case) · ★1)
